Abstract: True video intelligence demands more than recognizing what is visible: it requires reasoning about why events unfold, predicting what would change under different conditions, and deciding what to do next. We refer to this progression, from perception through causal reasoning and simulation to strategic planning, as Strategic Video Intelligence (SVI). No existing benchmark evaluates this capability stack: in-the-wild videos lack verifiable ground truth for causal and strategic questions, while synthetic environments sacrifice the complexity of real multi-agent systems. To bridge this gap, we introduce SVI-Bench, a large-scale benchmark that leverages team sports as a dynamic microworld, combining the complexity of real-world multi-agent interaction (10-22 agents making coordinated decisions under adversarial pressure) with the verifiability of explicit rules and definitive outcomes. SVI-Bench comprises approximately 35K hours of broadcast video, 15M annotated actions, 15K hours of expert commentary, 23K game reports, and 103K structured statistical records across basketball, soccer, and hockey, all constructed via a data engine that transforms raw game data into a dense, cross-referenced corpus. We organize evaluation into 9 tasks spanning a progressive four-pillar hierarchy: Dynamic Scene Understanding, Causal Reasoning, Strategic Simulation, and Agentic Synthesis. Evaluating strong multimodal and agentic baselines, we find a capability cliff: models perform competently on perceptual tasks, achieving approximately 73% on fine-grained action QA, but degrade sharply at each successive cognitive level. Agentic tasks prove hardest: the strongest model achieves only 5% accuracy when required to autonomously gather and integrate evidence across a corpus of 1.8M clips.

</details>


### [112] [Automated Prediction of Postoperative Pancreatic Fistula Using Preoperative Computed Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31539)
*Ashok Choudhary,Chris Varghese,Leo Y. Li-Han,Frank G. Lee,Ellen L. Larson,Elizabeth B. Habermann,Cornelius A. Thiels,Hojjat Salehinejad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的全自动管道，从CT扫描中的胰腺分割到分类，用于术前预测和分层术后胰瘘（POPF）风险。在包含自动分割胰腺体积和手术结果的数据集上，评估了多种3D CNN架构（CNN3D、R(2+1)D ResNet-18、ResNet-MC3-18），显示出有前景的预测性能，为胰腺手术的术前决策提供了临床工具和方法基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术后胰瘘（POPF）是胰腺切除术后严重的并发症，会增加发病率、住院时间和医疗成本，因此需要一种术前无创的风险评估工具来辅助临床决策。

Method: 提出了一种端到端的深度学习管道，包括自动胰腺分割和基于3D CNN的分类模块。使用了包含自动分割胰腺体积和手术结果的数据集，评估了三种3D架构：自定义轻量级CNN3D基线、R(2+1)D ResNet-18和ResNet-MC3-18。

Result: 多种3D架构在术前POPF风险预测上均显示出有前景的预测性能。

Conclusion: 该管道为胰腺特异性CT分类提供了临床有价值的工具和方法基准，有助于改善胰腺手术的术前决策。

Abstract: Postoperative pancreatic fistula (POPF) is a serious complication after pancreatic resection, increasing morbidity, hospital stay, and healthcare costs. We present an automatic, end-to-end deep learning pipeline-from pancreatic segmentation to classification-for preoperative POPF risk estimation and stratification using preoperative CT scans. A data set with auto-segmented pancreas volumes and surgical outcomes was used to evaluate multiple architectures, including a custom lightweight 3D CNN baseline (CNN3D), R(2+1)D ResNet-18, and ResNet-MC3-18 models. Evaluation across multiple 3D architectures demonstrated promising predictive performance. This approach offers a clinically valuable tool and a methodological benchmark for pancreas-specific CT classification, supporting improved preoperative decision-making in pancreatic surgery.

</details>


### [113] [SMART: SMPLest-X Mesh Adaptation and RAFT Tracking for Soccer Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31551)
*Parthsarthi Rawat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SMART方法，基于SMPLest-X微调，结合光流追踪、足部锚定和时序平滑，在FIFA骨骼追踪挑战赛中将3D姿态估计误差从1.053降至0.647（验证集），测试集Global MPJPE为0.324米。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在广播视频中估计足球运动员3D世界空间姿态的精度不足，需要更鲁棒的多任务学习框架来处理运动模糊、遮挡和快速运动等挑战。

Method: 1) 基于SMPLest-X (ViT-H, 687M参数)微调；2) 分层剪辑拆分策略；3) 多任务深度监督；4) 广播数据增强；5) RAFT密集光流相机追踪器；6) 足部平面锚定；7) 两遍时序平滑。

Result: 验证集从1.053降至0.647（提升38.6%）；测试集得分0.593，Global MPJPE 0.324米，Local MPJPE 0.054米。

Conclusion: SMART在FIFA官方标准下显著超越基线，证明了结合大模型微调、多任务学习和几何约束的有效性。

Abstract: We present our approach to the FIFA Skeletal Tracking Challenge 2026, which requires estimating 3D world-space poses of soccer players from broadcast video. Our method finetunes SMPLest-X (ViT-H, 687 M parameters) via a stratified clip split, multi-task depth supervision, and broadcast augmentation, paired with a RAFT dense optical flow camera tracker, foot-plane anchoring, and two-pass temporal smoothing. Against the FIFA baseline score of 1.053 on the validation set, SMART achieves 0.647, a 38.6% improvement; on the held-out test set, SMART scores 0.593 (Global MPJPE: 0.324 m, Local MPJPE: 0.054 m).

</details>


### [114] [EGOSTREAM: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Streaming Episodic Memory in Egocentric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31557)
*Rosario Forte,Giuseppe Lando,Antonino Furnari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了EgoStream，一个用于评估流式视频中情景记忆的诊断性基准，包含2250个问题、7个认知维度和答案有效期（AVW）机制。通过统一框架评测多种记忆管理机制，发现顶级方法仅约45%准确率，且远未达到实时处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前流式视频基准缺乏诊断模型记忆内容和记忆时长的工具，无法区分模型真正遗忘与环境自然变化导致的答案失效。

Method: 构建EgoStream基准（2250个问题，7个认知维度），引入答案有效期（AVW）扩展为8528个召回条件评估；在统一Qwen3-VL框架下对比滑动窗口、注意力汇、KV缓存剪枝/合并/卸载等记忆管理机制。

Result: 最高方法仅约45%准确率；所有方法远低于实时处理（>1秒/帧）；不同机制展现出完全不同的记忆剖面（如token剪枝优于合并保留细节，量化卸载改善超长期记忆）。

Conclusion: EgoStream揭示了现有架构在流式情景记忆方面的关键差距，为未来研究提供了必要的诊断测试平台。

Abstract: Continuous episodic memory is a core capability for autonomous agents operating in dynamic, real-world environments, yet current streaming video benchmarks provide limited tools for diagnosing what models remember and for how long. We introduce \egostream, a diagnostic benchmark for streaming episodic memory evaluation in egocentric vision. \egostream organizes 2,250 curated questions along seven cognitive dimensions: detail, spatial, temporal, event, social, causal, and prospective memory. We introduce the Answer Validity Window (AVW), which specifies the temporal span an answer remains valid as the observed scene evolves. This allows us to expand the questions into 8,528 recall-conditioned evaluations, enabling controlled testing from instant to ultra-long-term recall while separating genuine model forgetting from natural world-state changes. We rigorously establish baseline performance through a unified streaming MLLM framework that compares several state-of-the-art memory-management mechanisms, covering sliding windows, attention sinks, KV-cache pruning, merging, and offloading. Experiments within a unified Qwen3-VL backbone reveal that comparable aggregate accuracies mask starkly different memory profiles. For instance, token pruning preserves fine-grained details and temporal structure significantly better than token merging, while quantized offloading rescues ultra-long-term recall. Ultimately, all mechanisms operate well below real-time (>1s per frame), and top performing methods ceil at about 45\% accuracy, exposing critical gaps in current architectures. \egostream provides the diagnostic testbed needed to close these gaps.

</details>


### [115] [nuReasoning: A Reasoning-Centric Dataset and Benchmark for Long-Tail Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31572)
*Zhiyu Huang,Johnson Liu,Rui Song,Zewei Zhou,Ruining Yang,Yun Zhang,Tianhui Cai,Hanyin Zhang,Mingxuan Gao,Valeria Xu,Jiali Chen,Yishan Shen,Yiluan Guo,Tony,Qi,Jiaqi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: nuReasoning是一个大规模真实世界数据集和基准，专注于长尾驾驶场景中的推理能力，包含2万个20秒长的片段，覆盖空间推理、决策推理和反事实推理标注，实验表明在该数据集上微调可显著提升驾驶问答和规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶数据集主要关注感知、预测或规划，缺乏对现实长尾驾驶场景中推理过程的充分监督，而推理（常识知识、空间关系、智能体交互、安全决策）对长尾场景至关重要。

Method: 构建nuReasoning数据集：继承nuScenes和nuPlan框架，收集20,000个20秒长的驾驶片段（多城市、多摄像头、LiDAR、高清地图、目标标注），并加入人工验证的三种推理标注（空间推理、决策推理、反事实推理），同时支持推理评估和规划评估。

Result: 微调VLM显著提升驾驶特定问答能力；将推理监督融入VLA训练可提升规划性能，即使在推理时禁用文本推理输出仍有效。

Conclusion: nuReasoning为在现实长尾场景中评估和改进鲁棒、可解释、推理驱动的自动驾驶系统奠定了基准。

Abstract: Reasoning is essential for autonomous driving (AD) in long-tail scenarios, where vehicles must apply commonsense knowledge, understand spatial relations, infer agent interactions, and make safe decisions. However, existing AD datasets and benchmarks mainly target perception, prediction, or planning, and provide limited supervision for reasoning over realistic long-tail driving scenes. We introduce nuReasoning, a large-scale real-world dataset and benchmark for reasoning-centric AD. Following the lineage of nuScenes and nuPlan, nuReasoning advances real-world AD datasets and benchmarks toward reasoning in long-tail driving scenarios. The dataset contains 20,000 clips, each 20 seconds long, collected across multiple cities, with synchronized multi-camera images, LiDAR data, HD maps, object annotations, and human-verified reasoning annotations spanning Spatial Reasoning, Decision Reasoning, and Counterfactual Reasoning. Unlike prior datasets that focus primarily on visual question answering, nuReasoning supports both reasoning evaluation and planning evaluation, enabling a direct study of how reasoning supervision affects driving performance. Experiments show that fine-tuning VLMs on nuReasoning substantially improves driving-specific question answering, while incorporating reasoning supervision into VLA training improves planning performance even when textual reasoning outputs are disabled at inference time. These results establish nuReasoning as a foundation for evaluating and improving robust, interpretable, reasoning-driven AD systems in realistic long-tail settings.

</details>


### [116] [Joint Multi-Camera LiDAR Extrinsic Calibration via Learned Pairwise Initialization and Geometric Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31576)
*Aziz Al-Najjar,Marzieh Amini,James R. Green,Felix Kwamena*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段框架，先将CMRNext独立应用于每个相机生成初始外参和稠密2D-3D匹配，再通过联合光束法平差进行全局优化，实现多相机-激光雷达外参的联合标定，在KITTI和Walkley数据集上显著提升精度和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的相机-激光雷达标定方法独立处理每对相机-LiDAR，忽略了多相机平台中相机间的刚性几何耦合，导致单个相机标定结果在系统层面不一致。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段用CMRNext独立估计每个相机的初始外参并生成稠密2D-3D对应点；第二阶段通过多帧光束法平差，联合优化重投影误差、单相机先验和相对位姿先验，将独立预测转化为全局一致的多相机标定结果。

Result: KITTI数据集上平移误差0.89 cm、旋转误差0.038°；Walkley数据集上平移误差从108.6 cm降至3.1 cm，证明了多相机耦合在单相机预测不可靠时的显著优势。

Conclusion: 显式建模多相机间的几何耦合能有效提升标定的全局一致性和鲁棒性，尤其在单相机标定结果不稳定的场景下效果显著。

Abstract: Most learning-based camera-LiDAR calibration methods treat each camera-LiDAR pair independently, ignoring the rigid geometric coupling in multi-camera platforms. As a result, per-camera estimates may be individually accurate yet inconsistent at the system level. We present a two-stage framework for joint multi-camera LiDAR extrinsic calibration that combines learned pairwise matching with geometric refinement. First, CMRNext is applied independently to each camera to produce initial extrinsic estimates and dense 2D-3D correspondences. These predictions are then jointly refined through a multi-frame bundle adjustment with reprojection, per-camera prior, and relative-pose prior terms. This approach converts pairwise predictions into a globally consistent multi-camera calibration. Experiments on KITTI (in-domain for CMRNext) and Walkley (out-of-domain) datasets show improved per-camera accuracy and inter-camera consistency. On KITTI, the method achieves 0.89 cm translation error and 0.038 rotation error. On Walkley, it reduces translation error from 108.6 cm to 3.1 cm, highlighting the benefit of explicit multi-camera coupling when single-camera predictions are less reliable.

</details>


### [117] [SurGe: Improved Surface Geometry in Point Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31577)
*Karim Knaebel,Gonzalo Martin Garcia,Christian Schmidt,Ilya Fradlin,Lucas Nunes,Daan de Geus,Bastian Leibe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SurGe提出点图法向量指标来评估局部表面几何质量，并通过点梯度匹配损失和邻域注意力解码器（NAD）改进3D重建的局部几何精度，在8个零样本单目几何基准上取得最佳平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3D重建方法全局几何估计良好，但局部表面几何不准确，这在可视化上明显却难以被常见指标反映。作者希望设计一种更敏感的评估指标并改进局部几何质量。

Method: 1) 提出点图法向量指标（point map normal metric），通过分析邻近3D预测点诱导的局部表面方向来评估局部几何；2) 提出点梯度匹配损失（point gradient matching loss），监督深度归一化的3D有限差分；3) 提出邻域注意力解码器（Neighborhood Attention Decoder, NAD），逐步上采样特征并使用邻域注意力进行局部特征混合。

Result: 在8个零样本单目几何基准上，SurGe在全局点图AbsRel上取得最佳平均排名，并在局部点图和点图法向量评估上持续改进。

Conclusion: 通过显式关注局部几何质量，SurGe在保持全局精度的同时显著提升了局部表面重建的准确性。

Abstract: Recent feedforward 3D reconstruction methods predict point maps and estimate global 3D geometry remarkably well. However, their predictions still exhibit inaccurate local surface geometry, which is clearly visible qualitatively but only weakly reflected in common metrics. To make these errors more explicit in evaluation, we introduce a point map normal metric that evaluates the local surface orientation induced by neighboring 3D predictions. To reduce these errors, we propose two complementary components: a point gradient matching loss that supervises depth-normalized 3D finite differences, and a Neighborhood Attention Decoder (NAD) that progressively upsamples features and uses Neighborhood Attention for local feature mixing. Across eight zero-shot monocular geometry benchmarks, our model, SurGe, achieves the best average rank for global point map AbsRel and consistently improves local point map and point map normal evaluations.

</details>


### [118] [Recognizing Co-Speech Gestures in-the-Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31589)
*Sindhu B Hegde,K R Prajwal,Andrew Zisserman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了GRW数据集，这是首个大规模、帧级别标注的共语手势识别基准，涵盖150个词汇类别和156,688个视频片段，用于训练模型完成语义手势分类、手势词识别和时序定位三个任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态模型难以捕捉与语义相关的共语手势，主要受限于缺乏精确标注的训练数据。作者旨在填补这一空白，建立大规模、精细标注的手势识别基准数据集。

Method: 构建了GRW数据集，包含156,688个手动标注的视频片段，覆盖150个词汇的多样化分类体系（物理动作、空间描述、抽象概念）。基于该数据集训练视频模型，完成三个任务：(a) 语义手势分类，(b) 手势对应词识别，(c) 手势时序定位。

Result: 成功建立了GRW大规模基准数据集，并基于此数据集为三个手势识别任务提供了基准测试结果。

Conclusion: GRW数据集为共语手势识别提供了重要的训练资源和评估基准，有望推动多模态模型对语义手势的理解能力。

Abstract: While humans naturally gesture during speech, only a sparse subset of these movements are visually depictive and semantically linked to specific spoken words. Current multimodal models struggle to capture these semantic co-speech gestures, heavily bottlenecked by a lack of precisely annotated training data. To address this, we introduce the Gesture Recognition in the Wild (GRW) dataset, the first large-scale benchmark designed to map unconstrained human gestures to specific words with frame-accurate temporal boundaries. Comprising 156,688 manually annotated video clips, GRW spans a highly diverse 150-word taxonomy of physical actions, spatial descriptors, and abstract concepts. We leverage GRW to train video models to (a) classify gestures as semantic or not, (b) recognize the word corresponding to a co-speech gesture, and (c) temporally localize the gesture. We also use GRW to establish benchmarks for these three tasks.

</details>


### [119] [CoFiDA-M: Concept-Aware Feature Modulation for Cross-Domain Adaptation with Image-Only Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31591)
*Nurjahan Sultana,Moi Hoon Yap,Xinqi Fan,Wenqi Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoFiDA-M提出了一种特权信息框架，在训练时利用MONET基础模型提供的临床概念概率作为指导，通过教师网络和学生蒸馏，最终部署为仅需图像的模型，显著提升了皮肤癌筛查从专家级皮肤镜图像到消费者级临床图像的跨域性能，尤其在黑色素瘤召回率上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于AI的皮肤癌筛查模型在从专家级皮肤镜图像迁移到消费者级临床图像时性能严重下降，现有域适应方法忽略了临床概念等关键语义不变信息，而MONET等基础模型虽能提供语义概率信息，但测试时无法使用，形成部署悖论。

Method: 提出CoFiDA-M框架：1）训练时利用MONET的概念概率作为特权信息，指导教师网络通过FiLM调制器将视觉特征转换为语义"编辑"后的特征空间；2）通过蒸馏训练轻量级学生网络，使其不仅模仿教师的最终预测，更复现语义编辑后的中间表征，将临床推理"烘焙"进学生权重；3）部署时学生网络仅需图像输入。

Result: 在多数据集基准上，仅需图像的轻量级学生模型显著优于当前最先进方法，尤其在黑色素瘤召回率方面表现突出，展现出强大的跨数据集鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CoFiDA-M提供了一种实用且通用的框架，能有效利用带噪声的概率元数据作为特权信息，为皮肤癌筛查及其他领域向真实世界部署迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Models for AI-based skin cancer screening suffer a severe performance drop when shifting from expert dermoscopic (source) images to consumer-grade clinical (target) images, hindering real-world deployment. Existing domain adaptation methods often ignore crucial semantic invariants, such as clinical concepts. While new foundation models like MONET can provide this semantic information as dense, probabilistic scores, this metadata is unavailable at test time, creating a deployment paradox for practical image-only screening tools. We address this gap by proposing CoFiDA-M, a privileged information framework that learns from concepts at training time but deploys as an image-only model. Our method trains a teacher network that uses MONET concept probabilities to guide a FiLM modulator, transforming visual features into a semantically ``edited" feature space. A lightweight, image-only student is then trained to reproduce this edited representation, not just the teacher's final predictions. This distillation ``bakes" the clinical reasoning into the student's weights. On a challenging multi-dataset benchmark, our image-only student significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, especially in melanoma recall. Our work provides a practical and generalizable framework for leveraging noisy, probabilistic metadata as privileged information, demonstrating strong cross-dataset robustness and potential for real-world deployment beyond dermatology. Implementation code is available at: https://github.com/mmu-dermatology-research/CoFiDA.git

</details>


### [120] [Learning Global Motion with Compact Gaussians for Feed-Forward 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31595)
*Mungyeom Kim,Minkyeong Jeon,Honggyu An,Jaewoo Jung,Hyuna Ko,Jisang Han,Hyeonseo Yu,Donghwan Shin,Sunghwan Hong,Takuya Narihira,Kazumi Fukuda,Yuki Mitsufuji,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: C4G提出了一种基于紧凑型可学习高斯查询令牌的前馈式4D重建框架，通过时间条件建模全局运动，无需逐场景优化，并引入视频扩散模型增强渲染，在少高斯数、无需相机位姿的条件下实现强新视角合成与运动建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式方法逐帧预测3D高斯，存在高斯重复和视角依赖偏差问题，难以有效学习场景运动。

Method: 提出C4G框架，使用时间戳条件的紧凑可学习高斯查询令牌，在全时间上下文中聚合特征并解码3D高斯；引入视频扩散模型增强渲染；将特征提升为4D特征场以支持点跟踪等任务。

Result: 在显著减少高斯数且无需相机位姿的条件下，实现强新视角合成性能，展现出更强的运动建模能力和对大时间间隔的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: C4G通过紧凑查询令牌和全局运动建模，有效解决前馈式4D重建中的关键问题，在效率和性能上均取得优越表现。

Abstract: Dynamic scene reconstruction from monocular video remains a fundamental challenge in computer vision. Existing feed-forward methods predict 3D Gaussians pixel-wise for each frame, suffering from duplicated Gaussians and view-dependent biases that hinder effective learning of scene motion. We present C4G, a feed-forward 4D reconstruction framework built upon a compact set of timestamp-conditioned learnable Gaussian query tokens. Each token aggregates corresponding features across the full temporal context and decodes a 3D Gaussian whose position is modulated by the target timestamp, enabling globally coherent motion modeling without per-scene optimization. To capture fine-grained details, we further introduce a video diffusion model-based rendering enhancement module. Since our framework effectively aggregates features into Gaussians, we extend this capability to feature lifting, producing a 4D feature field that supports point tracking and dynamic scene understanding. C4G achieves strong novel-view synthesis performance using significantly fewer Gaussians and without requiring camera poses, while exhibiting stronger motion modeling and robustness to large temporal gaps.

</details>


### [121] [KLIP: localized distribution shift detection via KL-divergence with diffusion priors in Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31596)
*Alireza Kheirandish,Jihoon Hong,Sara Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于KL散度的OOD检测指标，无需校准数据或先验知识，可在逆问题中检测并定位图像中的分布偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型的OOD检测方法通常需要偏移分布的某些知识，难以检测细微或局部的分布偏移，且仅能处理完整图像而非逆问题中的间接测量值。

Method: 利用扩散先验与后验分布之间的Kullback-Leibler散度作为OOD检测指标，无需校准数据或偏移分布知识，同时支持整图检测和局部区域定位。

Result: 该方法能检测出语义上有意义的细微分布偏移（如健康肝脏CT与肿瘤肝脏CT的区分），并能在多种扩散模型、数据集和逆问题场景下泛化。

Conclusion: 所提KL散度指标有效解决了逆问题中OOD检测的多个痛点，具有通用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Diffusion models have shown promising performance as data-driven priors for computational imaging, as well as some capacity to detect out-of-distribution (OOD) images. However, existing approaches to OOD detection often require some knowledge of the shifted distribution, fail to detect subtle or localized distribution shifts, and operate on full images, rather than the indirect measurements available in inverse problems. We propose an OOD detection metric based on the Kullback-Leibler divergence between the diffusion prior and the posterior distribution, that (i) does not require any calibration data or knowledge of the shifted distribution, and (ii) can detect whole images as OOD as well as localize OOD patches within an image. Experimentally, we show that this metric can detect subtle yet semantically meaningful distribution shifts, such as the shift from healthy liver CT scans to those with tumors, and generalizes across different types of diffusion models, datasets, and inverse problems. Our code can be found at https://github.com/voilalab/KLIP.

</details>


### [122] [SOCO: Benchmarking Semantic Object Correspondence in Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31597)
*Olaf Dünkel,Basavaraj Sunagad,Haoran Wang,David T. Hoffmann,Christian Theobalt,Adam Kortylewski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SOCO基准，系统评估视觉基础模型的语义对应能力，包含100个类别、超100万对应点对和语言描述，揭示模型在跨类别对应、多模态对齐和下游任务预测上的关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉基础模型的结构化物体理解评估缺乏统一协议和部件级监督，亟需一个系统、一致的基准来评测语义对应能力。

Method: 构建SOCO基准，建立对应类型分类法，提供跨100类、超100万对的语义一致关键点标注，并引入语言描述以支持大视觉语言模型评估。

Result: (i) 视觉基础骨干编码强语义结构但跨类别对应差；(ii) LVLMs文本定位强于视觉跨图像匹配；(iii) 对应性能比ImageNet分类更能预测下游稠密任务表现。

Conclusion: SOCO可作为评估视觉与多模态基础模型结构化部件级表示质量的基准，揭示了当前模型在细粒度视觉对应上的关键局限。

Abstract: Measuring structured object understanding in vision foundation models remains challenging due to inconsistent evaluation protocols and limited part-level supervision. Semantic correspondence (SC) evaluates this capability by testing whether object parts can be matched across instances and categories under large variations in appearance, viewpoint, and geometry. To enable a systematic SC evaluation, we introduce SOCO, a new benchmark for Semantic Object Correspondence that introduces a taxonomy of correspondence types and provides consistent, functionally meaningful keypoint annotations across 100 categories and over 1M correspondence pairs. In addition, SOCO includes keypoint language descriptions, enabling the evaluation of large vision-language models (LVLMs) and their fine-grained part-level understanding. Comprehensive experiments reveal that (i) vision foundation backbones encode strong semantic structure but transfer correspondences poorly across related categories and only partially capture object-part position, (ii) LVLMs are stronger at text-prompted part localization than at visual-reference cross-image matching, exposing a gap between language-grounded localization and fine-grained visual correspondence, and (iii) correspondence performance predicts performance on dense downstream tasks, including segmentation, tracking, 3D pose estimation, and 3D detection, more strongly than ImageNet classification. Together, these findings position SOCO as a benchmark for structured, part-level representation quality in vision and multimodal foundation models.

</details>


### [123] [Linear Scaling Video VLMs for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31598)
*Cristobal Eyzaguirre,Jiajun Wu,Juan Carlos Niebles*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StateKV是一种推理时方法，通过固定容量的重要性驱动循环状态携带跨帧上下文，将预训练的长视频VLM适配为线性时间视频预填充，在保持接近全自注意力精度的同时显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频VLM采用时空自注意力导致计算量与帧数呈二次方增长，而现有的效率方法（激进丢帧/粗略注意力近似）虽提升可扩展性但损失了准确性。

Method: 提出StateKV，包含两个组件：(1) 固定容量、基于重要性的循环状态用于跨帧上下文传递，实现线性时间预填充；(2) 第二个全帧缓存用于解码。无需微调或架构修改。

Result: 在三个长视频基准测试、七种模型（涵盖三个家族和多种规模）上，StateKV保持接近全自注意力精度，一致优于滑动窗口/基于最近性的流式近似，并在相同计算预算下支持更大模型以提高准确率。

Conclusion: StateKV提供了一种实用且高效的长视频理解方法，在不牺牲精度的情况下实现了线性时间扩展，为可扩展的长视频理解迈出了实际一步。

Abstract: Video vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in long-horizon and streaming settings, yet most video encoders still rely on spatiotemporal self-attention, causing compute and latency to grow quadratically with the number of frames. Existing efficiency methods improve scalability but often lose accuracy relative to full self-attention, for example through aggressive frame/token dropping or coarse attention approximations. We introduce StateKV, an inference-time method that adapts pretrained long-video VLMs to linear-time video prefill by carrying cross-frame context in a fixed-capacity, importance-based recurrent state, paired with a second full per-frame cache used for decoding. Across three long-video benchmarks and seven models spanning three families and multiple scales, StateKV remains close to full self-attention and consistently outperforms dominant sliding-window / recency-based streaming approximations, without fine-tuning or architectural changes. StateKV also reduces video-prefill cost measured FLOPs, enabling stronger accuracy at a fixed compute budget by running larger models. These results suggest a practical step toward scalable long-video understanding.

</details>


### [124] [Representation Forcing for Bottleneck-Free Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31604)
*Yuqing Wang,Zhijie Lin,Ceyuan Yang,Yang Zhao,Fei Xiao,Hao He,Qi Zhao,Zihan Ding,Fuyun Wang,Shuai Wang,Youliang Zhang,Haoqi Fan,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Representation Forcing (RF) 技术，使统一多模态模型无需依赖外部VAE，通过让解码器自回归预测视觉表示作为中间token，再用这些token指导像素扩散，实现了无瓶颈的端到端图像生成与理解，效果媲美甚至超越基于VAE的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型依赖冻结的、预训练的VAE进行图像生成，这带来了结构性的瓶颈。若直接移除VAE，模型从原始像素同时学习高层结构和低层细节会导致质量下降。因此需要一种技术来弥合这一质量差距。

Method: 提出Representation Forcing (RF)：解码器先自回归预测视觉表示作为中间token，这些token保留在上下文中，在同一骨干网络内引导后续的像素扩散过程。将视觉表示从感知输出转变为生成目标，从而消除对外部生成潜在空间的需求。

Result: 在图像生成上，使用RF的像素空间模型匹配了最先进的基于VAE的统一模型；在图像理解上，像素空间RF普遍优于其基于VAE的变体。

Conclusion: RF为构建无瓶颈的端到端统一多模态模型提供了有效的一步，无需依赖外部VAE即可在理解和生成任务上取得优异表现。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) aim to handle perception and generation in a single model. Yet existing UMMs still rely on a frozen, separately pretrained VAE for image generation, imposing a structural bottleneck. Naively removing it introduces a quality gap, as the model must learn both high-level structure and low-level details from raw pixels. In this paper, we propose Representation Forcing (RF), a technique that closes this gap by making representation prediction a native capability of the model. Concretely, RF forces the decoder to autoregressively predict visual representations as intermediate tokens before pixels; these tokens then stay in context to guide pixel diffusion within the same backbone. By turning representations from perception outputs into generation targets, RF eliminates the need for any external generative latent space. We find that RF benefits both understanding and generation. On image generation, our pixel-space model with RF matches state-of-the-art VAE-based unified models. On image understanding, pixel-space RF generally outperforms its VAE-based variant. Together, these results offer an effective step toward end-to-end, bottleneck-free UMMs.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Structured interactions improve distributed coordination beyond model scaling in a real-world multi-robot system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30383)
*Junping Wang,Zhizhong Zhang,Yongqiang Tang,Geng Zheng,Jiaming Zhang,Shiji Song,Yanmei Li,Yushan Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究在10台物理机器人上比较了通信拓扑结构调整与模型规模扩大对多机器人系统性能的影响，发现将全连接改为分层层次化交互可使性能提升47分，而加倍神经网络隐藏层大小最多仅提升9分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在固定硬件预算下，重新组织机器人间通信结构是否比单纯扩大单机模型规模带来更大的性能收益。

Method: 使用10台实体机器人执行运输与地图构建任务（每种条件5次，共60次实验），对比全连接拓扑与模块化层次化拓扑，同时对比不同神经网络隐藏层大小，采用嵌套混合效应模型进行统计分析，并在SMAC仿真环境中进行独立复现验证。

Result: 通信拓扑从全连接变为层次化交互使标准化性能提升47分（0-100分制），而加倍隐藏层大小最多仅提升9分；嵌套混合效应模型显示拓扑对模型拟合的改善显著大于规模；超过1024隐藏单元时性能出现饱和。

Conclusion: 在测试的系统与任务设定下，交互结构对性能的主导作用远超模型规模，但更广泛的量化泛化结论仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Scaling individual robot capabilities is common but costly. Here we investigate a system-level design question in real-world multi-robot coordination: given matched hardware budgets, does restructuring communication among robots yield larger gains than increasing onboard model size? Using a representative transport-and-mapping task with 10 physical robots (5 runs per condition, 60 runs total), we find that switching from fully connected to modular hierarchical interactions improves normalised performance by 47 points (0--100), whereas doubling neural network hidden size yields at most 9 points. Nested mixed-effects model comparisons show a substantially larger improvement in model fit for topology than for scale. The pattern is confirmed in independent SMAC replications; heterogeneous benchmark reanalyses provide secondary supporting consistency checks rather than primary evidence. Performance saturation beyond 1024 hidden units is observed in simulation-calibrated extrapolation, not directly on hardware. These results indicate that interaction structure can play a dominant role within the tested system and task setting, while broader quantitative generalisation remains to be established.

</details>


### [126] [Learning-Based Navigation for Indoor Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30468)
*Tri-Tin Nguyen,Tien-Dat Nguyen,Gia-Uy Le,Vinh Nguyen,Vinh-Hao Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合监督学习全局规划器和经PPO优化的DWA局部规划器的室内移动机器人导航框架，在仿真和真实环境中验证了其生成可行全局路径和可靠局部运动指令的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内移动机器人导航方法中，传统规划器在复杂场景下难以兼顾全局路径的可行性与局部控制的鲁棒性，亟需一种能有效融合学习与优化的导航框架。

Method: 采用监督学习训练基于代价感知A*专家轨迹的神经网络全局规划器；局部规划器基于DWA动作格网进行离散候选选择，先通过行为克隆初始化策略，再使用PPO算法结合可行性感知掩码进行精细化训练。

Result: 在仿真和真实室内环境中，该方法能够生成可行的全局路径和可靠的局部运动指令，实现有障碍物环境下的安全目标导向导航。

Conclusion: 将学习型全局规划与经强化学习优化的局部控制相结合，是提升室内移动机器人导航性能的有效途径。

Abstract: This paper presents a learning-based navigation framework for indoor mobile robots. The proposed method combines a supervised neural global planner, trained from cost-aware A* expert trajectories, with the proposed Learning-Based DWA local planner, which is formulated as discrete candidate selection over the Dynamic Window Approach (DWA) action lattice. For local planning, the policy is first trained by behavior cloning and then refined by Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) under feasibility-aware masking. The framework is implemented and evaluated in both simulated and real-world indoor environments. Experimental results show that the proposed method generates feasible global routes and reliable local motion commands for safe goal-directed navigation in the presence of obstacles. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of integrating learning-based global planning with reinforcement-learning-refined local control for indoor mobile robot navigation. The source code will be released at https://ntdathp.github.io/rl_robot_web/.

</details>


### [127] [ELAN4D: Embodiment-Centric 4D Supervision for Vision-Language-Action Models via Plug-and-Play Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30484)
*Zeyuan He,Bowen Yang,Zhirui Fang,Keru Zhou,Lei Jiang,Jingjing Qian,Fan Mo,Junchi Yan,Philip Torr,Xiu Li,Li Jiang,Jialin Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ELAN4D通过预测机器人关键点未来轨迹（4D监督信号）增强VLA策略的鲁棒性和泛化能力，仅使用正运动学信息，无需额外传感器，推理时零开销，在多项任务中取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型直接根据当前观测回归动作，缺乏对未来动态的显式建模，导致在分布外扰动（如相机、背景、布局变化）下泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出ELAN4D框架，利用正向运动学从本体感知状态中提取机器人关键点（关节、末端执行器）的3D位移轨迹作为4D时空监督信号，通过轻量级轨迹解码器以即插即用方式注入动作专家模块，同时通过梯度隔离保留预训练视觉-语言骨干网络，推理时丢弃解码器，不改变原始策略接口。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin2.0及真实世界操作任务中，ELAN4D一致优于强VLA基线，在分布外扰动（相机、背景、布局变化）下取得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 以具身为中心的4D监督信号能有效构建更鲁棒和泛化的操作策略，且该方法轻量、即插即用、推理无额外开销。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown promise for robotic manipulation, yet most existing policies operate reactively by directly regressing actions from current observations, without explicitly modeling future dynamics. This limits their ability to generalize under out-of-distribution perturbations. To address this issue, we propose ELAN4D, an embodiment-centric, 4D-aware training framework that enhances VLA policies with future robot keypoint tracks as predictive spatio-temporal supervision. Using only forward kinematics from proprioceptive states, we derive 3D displacement tracks of robot keypoints, such as joints and the end-effector, with negligible preprocess cost. These tracks provide metric and compact supervision without requiring external trackers or reconstruction. A plug-and-play auxiliary branch with a lightweight track decoder injects this 4D signal into the action expert while preserving the pretrained vision-language backbone through gradient isolation. The track decoder is discarded during inference, leaving the base policy interface unchanged. Extensive experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, RoboTwin2.0 and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that ELAN4D consistently improves over strong VLA baselines, achieving the best overall performance and substantial gains under out-of-distribution perturbations, including camera, background, and layout shifts. These results highlight the effectiveness of embodiment-centric 4D supervision for building more robust and generalizable manipulation policies.

</details>


### [128] [CoMo3R-SLAM: Collaborative Monocular Dense SLAM with Learned 3D Reconstruction Priors for Outdoor Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30488)
*Zhihao Cao,Qi Shao,Shuhao Zhai,Feng Tian,Anh Nguyen,Hesheng Wang,Baoru Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoMo3R-SLAM是首个协作式单目密集RGB SLAM系统，利用学习的前馈3D重建先验，实现多智能体在室外大场景下的实时稠密建图，无需深度传感器，性能媲美甚至超越RGB-D方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作式稠密SLAM依赖深度传感器，带来有效载荷、功耗和标定成本；单目RGB相机虽轻量，但尺度模糊和跨智能体数据关联不可靠，尤其在低重叠和重复结构的室外场景中传统特征匹配失效，亟需鲁棒的几何信息。

Method: 每个智能体运行先验引导前端（prior-guided front-end）实现实时跟踪与局部稠密融合；协调器执行稠密点图匹配（dense pointmap matching）进行跨智能体验证、闭式Sim(3)规范同步（closed-form Sim(3) gauge synchronization）以及GPU加速的全局光束法平差（global bundle adjustment）配合分段级深度优化。

Result: 在Tanks and Temples和Waymo序列上，CoMo3R-SLAM在四个场景中的三个取得了最佳ATE，Waymo精度具有竞争力，匹配或超越最先进RGB-D方法，且在线运行速度达8 FPS。

Conclusion: 首次证明仅凭单目RGB即可实现协作式稠密SLAM，产生鲁棒的跨智能体约束和全局一致的度量地图，为多机器人室外建图提供轻量级可行方案。

Abstract: Collaborative dense SLAM is essential for multi-robot teams to achieve scalable and consistent 3D perception across large-scale outdoor environments. Existing systems typically depend on depth sensors, incurring significant payload, power, and calibration costs. Monocular RGB cameras are a lightweight alternative, but collaborative monocular dense SLAM remains difficult due to scale ambiguity, unreliable inter-agent data association, especially in outdoor scenes where low overlap and repetitive structures make traditional feature matching unreliable, motivating robust geometric information. We propose CoMo3R-SLAM, the first collaborative monocular dense RGB SLAM system that leverages robust learned feed-forward 3D reconstruction priors for outdoor multi-agent mapping. Each agent runs a prior-guided front-end for real-time tracking and local dense fusion, while a coordinator performs dense pointmap matching for cross-agent verification, closed-form Sim(3) gauge synchronization, and GPU-accelerated global bundle adjustment with segment-level depth optimization. Requiring neither depth sensors nor parametric intrinsics, our system produces robust cross-agent constraints and globally consistent metric maps from monocular RGB alone. On Tanks and Temples and Waymo sequences, CoMo3R-SLAM achieves the best ATE on three of four Tanks and Temples scenes and competitive Waymo accuracy, matching or exceeding state-of-the-art RGB-D methods while running online at 8 FPS.

</details>


### [129] [Physics-informed Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning under Hybrid Contact Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30503)
*Vittorio Giammarino,Anastasios Manganaris,Ahmed H. Qureshi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发现现有的物理信息目标条件强化学习（Pi-GCRL）方法在接触丰富的操作任务中会退化，并提出了一种接触感知和分层的方法来有选择地应用物理信息归纳偏置，从而将Pi-GCRL拓展到接触丰富的操作领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Pi-GCRL方法在导航和无物体目标到达领域表现良好，但在接触丰富的任务中，由于接触交互导致的混合动力学、模式依赖的可控性和非平滑价值景观，这些方法的可靠性尚不清楚。作者通过分析发现直接应用现有方法会导致性能退化，因此需要为接触丰富的操作任务设计专门的方法。

Method: 提出接触感知和分层（contact-aware and hierarchical）的Pi-GCRL方法，在操作问题中有选择性地应用物理信息归纳偏置，而非像现有方法那样全局应用。

Result: 通过分析揭示了现有Pi-GCRL方法在接触丰富任务中退化的原因，并证明了所提出的接触感知和分层方法能够有效改进性能。

Conclusion: 这项工作为将Pi-GCRL方法拓展到接触丰富的操作任务提供了原则性的一步，证明了需要针对接触动力学特性设计专门的结构化归纳偏置。

Abstract: Learning to reach arbitrary goals from sparse feedback requires agents to infer a rich notion of reachability across state--goal pairs. Goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) tackles this challenge by learning policies that generalize across goals, but this generalization becomes increasingly difficult as the underlying dynamics become high-dimensional, hybrid, or contact-dependent. To address this issue, physics-informed GCRL (Pi-GCRL) introduces optimal-control-inspired inductive biases into goal-conditioned value learning. While Pi-GCRL methods have proven effective in navigation and object-free goal-reaching domains, their reliability in contact-rich tasks remains unclear, where contact interactions induce hybrid dynamics, mode-dependent controllability, and nonsmooth value landscapes. In this work, we show that these structural properties can cause existing Pi-GCRL methods to degrade when applied naively to contact-rich manipulation. Motivated by this analysis, we introduce contact-aware and hierarchical formulations that apply physics-informed inductive biases selectively across the manipulation problem. Our results provide a principled step toward extending Pi-GCRL to contact-rich manipulation.

</details>


### [130] [VLM-GLoc: Vision-Language Model Enhanced Monte Carlo Localization for Robust Semantic Global Localization in Cluttered Quasi-Static Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30506)
*Shivendra Agrawal,Bradley Hayes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VLM-GLoc，一种利用开放词汇视觉语言模型（VLM）进行分层语义蒙特卡洛定位的方法，在几何歧义且语义重复的室内环境（如杂货店、实验室）中实现了70%-74%的全局定位成功率，显著优于传统几何方法和领域特定视觉基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全局定位方法在几何歧义（如平行货架、重复家具）和长尾语义分布环境中表现不佳，而领域特定视觉管线难以处理视觉杂乱和语义模糊问题，亟需一种统一的、可泛化的语义观测前端来提升定位鲁棒性。

Method: 提出VLM-GLoc，核心包括：(1) 利用开放词汇VLM提取高判别性文本特征作为统一语义观测；(2) 隐式质量过滤模糊或动态物体；(3) 永久性推理以进行针对性数据增强；以及 (4) 逆语义提议机制，通过文本到地图检索播种粒子。

Result: 在两个真实环境（3500平方英尺杂货店+手机平台、3700平方英尺实验室+四足机器人）中，全局定位成功率分别为70%和74%，显著优于传统纯几何方法和领域特定基线。

Conclusion: VLM-GLoc证明了开放词汇VLM作为统一语义观测前端在解决几何歧义室内环境全局定位问题上的有效性，为移动机器人在语义重复场景中的可靠定位提供了新范式。

Abstract: Global localization in geometrically aliased, quasi-static environments such as grocery stores, offices, schools, and hospitals poses a significant challenge for mobile robots. Grocery stores with parallel aisles and a long tailed distribution of products, as well as offices and labs with repetitive furniture such as chairs, desks, monitors, and doors, exemplify common indoor environments that present geometric and even semantic ambiguity. Traditional approaches rely either on distinct geometric features or on domain-specific vision pipelines that struggle with long-tail semantic distributions and transient visual clutter. We present VLM-GLoc, a method for hierarchical semantic Monte Carlo Localization (MCL) that leverages open-vocabulary Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as a unified semantic observation front-end. We hypothesize a three-fold benefit from VLMs: (1) extracting highly discriminative rich text features, (2) implicit quality filtering of blurry or dynamic objects, and (3) permanence reasoning for targeted data augmentation. We introduce an inverse semantic proposal mechanism that seeds particles via text-to-map retrieval. Evaluated across two real-world environments with different characteristics and two different platforms: a 3,500 sq. ft. grocery store with a cellphone and a 3,700 sq. ft. lab space with a quadruped, VLM-GLoc achieves 70% and 74% global localization success respectively, substantially outperforming traditional geometry-only and domain-specific baselines.

</details>


### [131] [ARISTO Hand: Sensing-Driven Distal Hyperextension for Fine-Grained Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30508)
*Aaron Kim,Dong Ho Kang,Mark Helwig,Mingyo Seo,Kazuto Yokoyama,Tetsuya Narita,Luis Sentis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ARISTO Hand是一款腱驱动机器人手，通过主动远端过伸和混合指尖传感架构，在薄物体操控中实现2.76倍的拉力提升，并成功完成SD卡插拔任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿人机器手在操控薄物体时，缺乏精确的接触几何和可靠的力感知能力，难以完成精细操作。

Method: 提出ARISTO Hand：1) 腱驱动+主动远端过伸机制，超越标准屈曲运动学极限；2) 混合指尖传感架构：刚性指甲安装的力-扭矩传感器 + 软电容触觉阵列。

Result: 主动过伸使1-20mm厚度物体的拔出力提升2.76倍；刚性指甲传感器在边缘接触（运动学奇异点附近）仍能提供可靠的力测量。

Conclusion: 验证了主动过伸+混合传感架构在薄物体精细操控中的有效性，并通过SD卡多阶段提取与插入任务证明了实用性。

Abstract: Manipulating thin objects requires precise contact geometry and reliable force perception, yet many anthropomorphic robotic hands lack the mechanical and sensing capabilities needed for such interactions. We present the ARISTO Hand, a tendon-driven robotic hand that integrates active distal hyperextension with a hybrid fingertip-sensing architecture that combines a rigid, nail-mounted force-torque sensor and a soft capacitive tactile array. Active hyperextension enables controlled fingertip engagement beyond the kinematic limits of standard flexion, increasing pull-out force by 2.76x for object thicknesses of 1-20 mm while preserving the nominal grasp capability. The rigid nail-mounted sensor provides reliable force measurements during edge contacts, where the sensitivity of proprioceptive force estimation degrades as the contact geometry approaches kinematic singularities. We validate the proposed architecture through quantitative force characterization and a multi-stage SD card extraction and insertion task. Video and supplementary materials are available at: https://aristohand.github.io

</details>


### [132] [Any-ttach: Quick End-effector Swapping Enables Manipulation Dexterity with Simplicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30569)
*Weizhe Ni,Jinzhou Li,Haoyu Li,Cody Andres Alessio-Bunnell,Wenjing Pan,Xianyi Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Any-ttach提出了一种以工具为中心的灵巧操作框架，通过快速末端执行器切换替代复杂的多指手，实现低成本、高效的机器人操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类手的复杂性很大程度上是为了使用和制造工具，而非单纯追求灵巧性。因此，通过快速切换末端执行器/工具，可以以简单结构实现高灵巧度。

Method: 开发了低成本自动切换机构（适配于开闭式夹爪）、手持式示教数据采集设备，以及组合学习型、参数化、规划型工具技能的任务规划框架。

Result: Any-ttach提升了工具切换可靠性、示教效率，降低了工具位姿变异性，并支持多样化工具使用技能。在三明治制作和黄瓜准备两个长时任务中，成功通过末端切换执行了6个子技能。

Conclusion: 机器人的操作能力不仅可以通过更复杂的末端执行器来扩展，也可以通过快速可更换的工具和末端模块来增强。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation dexterity is often pursued by building increasingly complex high-DoF multifingered hands. While many robotic hands are designed to replicate human morphology, the functional role of human hands suggests a different perspective: much of their complexity may exist to enable tool use and tool making. This observation motivates Any-ttach, a tool-centric manipulation framework that treats quick end-effector swapping as a mechanism for dexterity with simplicity. Any-ttach combines a low-cost automatic swapping mechanism for an open-close robot interface, a handheld device for collecting human demonstrations, and a task planning framework that composes learned, parameterized, and planned tool-use skills. The system supports diverse tools and end-effector modules, including daily tools, articulated tools such as scissors, Fin Ray fingers, and a low-cost anthropomorphic hand, through the same shared interface. Our experiments show that Any-ttach improves tool-swapping reliability, increases demonstration efficiency, reduces tool-pose variability, and supports diverse tool-use skills. In two long-horizon tasks, making a sandwich and preparing a cucumber, Any-ttach executes six tool-use subskills through end-effector switching and execution monitoring. These results suggest that robots can expand manipulation capability not only through more complex end-effectors, but also through rapidly exchangeable tools and end-effector modules. More details and videos are available at https://any-ttach.github.io/.

</details>


### [133] [Caspar: CUDA Accelerator for Symbolic Programming with Adaptive Reordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30583)
*Emil Martens,Aaron Miller,Matias Varnum,Annette Stahl*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Caspar 是一个利用 GPU 加速机器人领域非线性优化的库，通过符号编程自动生成 CUDA 内核，在 Bundle Adjustment 任务上比现有最优方法快 5-20 倍，且内存消耗更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让现代 GPU 的强大算力在机器人领域更加易用，弥合 Python 符号编程与 C++ 高性能 GPU 运行时之间的鸿沟。

Method: 基于 SymForce 库，用户定义符号残差函数，Caspar 通过符号微分自动生成优化的 CUDA 内核，支持李群运算，并提供非线性优化求解器接口。

Result: 在 BAL 数据集上，Caspar 比现有最优 Bundle Adjustment 方法快 5-20 倍，内存需求更少，且精度相当。

Conclusion: 符号化 GPU 编程方法在机器人非线性优化中具有显著性能优势，Caspar 已作为 SymForce 的一部分开源发布。

Abstract: We present Caspar, a library that makes the power of modern GPUs more accessible in robotics and provides a state-of-the-art nonlinear GPU solver that can be applied to a wide range of different optimization problems. Caspar bridges the gap between expressive symbolic programming in Python and high-performance GPU runtimes in C++ by automatically generating optimized CUDA kernels from symbolic expressions. Building on the SymForce library, users can easily define and combine symbolic expressions, including Lie group operations, to generate custom CUDA kernels. To use Caspar as a solver, users need only define the symbolic residual functions; Caspar then uses symbolic differentiation to generate the necessary GPU kernels and interfaces to perform nonlinear optimization.
  In this paper, we present the core components of Caspar and showcase its performance by performing bundle adjustment on the Bundle Adjustment in the Large (BAL) dataset. We benchmark Caspar against other state-of-the-art bundle adjusters and show that it is 5 to 20 times faster than the best alternative, requires less memory, and achieves similar accuracy. This illustrates the benefit of our symbolic GPU programming approach. Caspar is released as part of SymForce and is freely available at https://github.com/symforce-org/symforce

</details>


### [134] [ZAPS-DA: Zero-Phase Action Policy Smoothing with Decoupled Actor for Continuous Control in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30612)
*Faiq Shamass*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ZAPS-DA通过零相位滤波的因果蒸馏，在不产生相位延迟和无需后处理的情况下，将强化学习策略的动作抖动降低14-45倍，同时保持任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于离策略强化学习训练的策略常产生高频动作抖动，无法直接部署到物理执行器。事后滤波会引入相位延迟，而嵌入平滑性惩罚会混淆RL梯度，导致过度平滑。

Method: ZAPS-DA框架：主actor保持原始RL损失不变，另用一个去耦actor通过监督学习模仿回放缓冲区中零相位滤波后的目标动作。部署时仅使用去耦actor，实现非因果滤波器的因果蒸馏。

Result: 在MetaDrive上：转向抖动降低14-21倍，油门抖动降低3-5倍（p<10^-4），任务完成率匹配，奖励成本仅6.3%。在Webots上：转向抖动降低8-45倍，任务失败率从2.0%降至0.7%，奖励无显著差异。

Conclusion: ZAPS-DA有效解耦了平滑性与RL优化目标，在不引入相位延迟和后处理的前提下显著降低动作抖动，且无需调参，具备跨优化器类的可迁移性。

Abstract: Continuous control policies trained with off-policy reinforcement learning frequently exhibit high-frequency action jitter, rendering direct deployment on physical actuators impractical. Post-hoc filtering attenuates jitter but introduces phase lag; embedding smoothness penalties in the actor's loss couples them with the RL gradient and conflates reward regression with over-aggressive smoothing. We present ZAPS-DA, a framework that reduces action jitter at deployment with negligible phase lag and no post-processing. ZAPS-DA pairs an unmodified main actor (trained by the base RL loss) with a separate decoupled actor trained via supervised imitation of zero-phase filtered targets stored in the replay buffer. The deployed policy is the decoupled actor: a feed-forward map from the current observation to a smooth action, with no inference-time filter and no action-history input -- a mechanism we term causal distillation of a non-causal filter. A magnitude-matched MSE loss provides zero-hyperparameter portability across optimizer classes. Validated with Soft Actor-Critic and a Savitzky--Golay filter in two driving simulators using paired n=150 evaluation protocols: on MetaDrive, ZAPS-DA reduces steering jitter by 14--21x and throttle jitter by 3--5x (all $p < 10^{-4}$, Bonferroni-corrected) while matching task-completion (p=0.28 success, p=0.31 crash) at a 6.3% reward cost; on a custom Webots adaptive cruise control environment, the same SG configuration produces a Pareto improvement -- reward parity (p=0.121), 8--45x steering jitter reduction, and total task-failure rate reduced from 2.0% to 0.7%.

</details>


### [135] [Exploiting Chordal Sparsity for Globally Optimal Estimation with Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30617)
*Avinash Subramanian,Connor Holmes,Timothy D. Barfoot,Frank Dellaert,Frederike Dümbgen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在GTSAM框架中实现了自动构建凸SDP松弛的全局求解器，并通过贝叶斯树分解加速，解决了局部极小值问题，同时保持了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准因子图求解器可能收敛到差的局部极小值，存在安全隐患；而凸松弛方法虽然能全局求解或认证，但公式化困难且求解SDP成本高。

Method: 1) 在GTSAM框架中自动为常见因子图构建凸SDP松弛；2) 利用GTSAM自带的贝叶斯树结构分解SDP问题，加速弦稀疏问题的求解。

Result: 在3D位姿图SLAM（环形图）和2D定位（链式图）两个案例中，展示了该结构感知的全局估计器相比局部求解器具有更好的扩展性。

Conclusion: 该方法在保证全局最优性的同时显著提升了计算效率，开源代码已发布。

Abstract: Robust and efficient state estimation is crucial for perception, navigation, and control in robotics. State estimation problems are conveniently modeled using the factor-graph framework as enabled by modern software packages such as GTSAM or g2o. However, the standard solvers included in such frameworks are local and may converge to poor local minima, posing significant safety concerns. Conversely, techniques based on convex relaxations have been shown to provide a means of globally solving or certifying many state estimation problems. However, these relaxations 1) often require substantial effort to formulate, and 2) may incur significantly higher cost compared to efficient local solvers, as they require solving a large semidefinite program (SDP). In this work, we address both shortcomings by 1) creating a new procedure within the GTSAM framework for automatically constructing convex SDP relaxations for any factor graphs with common factor and variable types, and by 2) exploiting the Bayes tree constructions native to GTSAM to decompose the SDP problem, leading to significant speedup in solver time for chordally sparse problems. We demonstrate the favorable scaling of this structure-exploiting global estimator compared to standard local solvers for two case studies: A 3D pose-graph SLAM problem with a ring factor graph and a 2D localization problem with a chain factor graph. The software framework is available at https://github.com/borglab/gtsam.

</details>


### [136] [Bidirectional Incremental Generalized Hybrid A*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30647)
*Sidharth Talia,Oren Salzman,Siddhartha Srinivasa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Bi-IGHA*算法，通过双向搜索扩展增量式广义混合A*（IGHA*），解决复杂动力学系统中冻结顶点隐藏解的问题，在R3到R6规划问题上显著减少搜索扩张，并保持单调代价改进和终止保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非结构化环境中，具有复杂动力学的系统进行高效任意时间运动规划时，预计算运动基元不可行。直接应用A*受维数诅咒计算量过大，Hybrid A*通过离散化代价较小但产生了树发现与离散化分辨率之间的耦合。IGHA*通过层次化分辨率搜索打破耦合，但冻结顶点可能隐藏解。作者希望进一步缓解这一问题。

Method: 将IGHA*扩展到双向搜索设置（Bi-IGHA*），从起点和终点同时进行层次化搜索，利用双向搜索天然减少搜索深度的优势，从根本上缓解冻结顶点隐藏解的问题。

Result: Bi-IGHA*在R3、R4和R6规划问题上大幅减少了搜索扩展次数；在高速越野自主驾驶的动力学规划中，以显著更少的扩展实现等效的闭环性能。

Conclusion: Bi-IGHA*保留了IGHA*的单调代价改进和终止保证，同时通过双向搜索更有效地处理冻结顶点问题，提升了搜索效率。

Abstract: We focus on the problem of efficient anytime kinodynamic planning for systems with complex dynamics in unstructured environments that make precomputing motion primitives infeasible. Directly applying A* to such problems is computationally infeasible due to the curse of dimensionality. Methods such as Hybrid A* addressed this burden by discretizing the state space, but in turn creating a coupling between tree discovery and the discretization resolution. The Incremental Generalized Hybrid A* (IGHA*) performs search over a hierarchy of resolutions in an anytime fashion to break this coupling, by freezing vertices to use in later search iterations rather than pruning them. However, the frozen vertices can hide solution-supporting vertices from the search at a particular iteration. While classical bidirectional search is motivated by the reduction of search depth, extending IGHA* into the bidirectional setting (termed Bi-IGHA*) obtains additional benefit by fundamentally mitigating the behaviour induced by frozen vertices hiding solutions. We show that Bi-IGHA* preserves IGHA*'s guarantees on monotonic cost improvement and termination. We empirically show that Bi-IGHA* substantially reduces expansions on R3, R4, and R6 planning problems, and achieves equivalent closed-loop performance with kinodynamic planning for high-speed off-road autonomy while requiring significantly fewer expansions. Website: https://personalrobotics.github.io/IGHAStar/biighastar.html

</details>


### [137] [Primitive Subspaces Mediate Few-Shot Transfer in VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30695)
*Anya Singh,Cabrel Happi,Jai Relan,Varun Nair,Vidyut Baradwaj*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向VLA策略的基元感知训练方法，通过构建可组合的子技能库实现少样本迁移。在接触密集型装配任务上，基元训练模型仅需3条示范即可达到微调上限性能的78%，比平面训练模型样本效率提升3倍。消融实验证实基元表征是迁移的因果必要条件。同时指出分块策略评估中单步动作范围门控的族系膨胀会导致虚假失败率虚高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA策略在工业环境中部署时，每个新任务都需要微调，成本高昂。研究者希望探索一种基元感知训练方法，使策略能够通过少量示范组合已学习的子技能，完成未训练过的任务，从而降低新任务的部署成本。

Method: 在OpenVLA和π₀.₅两种VLA架构上，使用REASSEMBLE接触密集型装配数据集，在匹配的LoRA微调配方和固定超参数下，比较平面轨迹训练与基元分割片段（配合基元特定语言提示）训练的效果。保留6个对象-任务组合，评估模型在0/1/3/5/10条示范下的少样本迁移性能。通过基元可解码子空间消融验证因果性，并识别分块策略评估的方法学陷阱。

Result: 基元训练模型仅需3条示范即可达到微调上限性能的78%，而平面训练模型需要10条示范才能达到同等水平——样本效率差距达3倍，并在多种种子、架构和数据集上一致复现。消融基元可解码子空间后迁移性能下降32个百分点，而消融等维随机子空间无影响。分块策略中单步动作范围门控的族系膨胀会导致虚假失败率被高估一个数量级。

Conclusion: 基元感知训练能够生成可迁移的子技能库，通过少量示范组合即可完成未见任务，显著提升VLA策略的少样本迁移效率。基元表征是迁移成功的因果必要条件。同时需要警惕分块策略评估中的方法学陷阱。

Abstract: Deploying vision-language-action (VLA) policies in industrial environments requires the ability to teach new tasks at low cost, a property current VLAs lack, since each new task requires fine-tuning. We investigate whether primitive-aware training produces a transferable artifact: a learned library of sub-skills that can be composed at inference time, conditioned on a small number of demonstrations, to perform tasks the policy was never trained on. We train two VLA architectures with different inductive biases, OpenVLA and $π_{0.5}$, on the REASSEMBLE contact-rich assembly dataset under matched LoRA fine-tuning recipes and locked hyperparameters, varying training between flat trajectories and primitive-segmented episodes with primitive-specific language prompts. We hold out 6 object-task combinations from training and evaluate few-shot transfer: models receive $m \in \{0, 1, 3, 5, 10\}$ demonstrations of a held-out task and attempt execution without weight updates. We replicate across three training seeds and validate on a second dataset (LIBERO-Long). Primitive-trained models reach 78% of fine-tuned upper-bound performance with only m=3 demonstrations, while flat-trained models require m=10 demonstrations to reach the same level -- a $3\times$ sample efficiency gap that replicates across seeds, architectures, and datasets. To establish causation, we ablate the primitive-decodable subspace of hidden states and show few-shot transfer degrades by 32 percentage points while ablating a random subspace of equal dimensionality has no effect, indicating primitive representations are causally necessary rather than incidentally correlated with transfer. We identify and correct a methodological pitfall in evaluating chunked policies: family-wise inflation of single-step action-range gates produces order-of-magnitude higher false-failure rates against ground-truth human demonstrations.

</details>


### [138] [Geometry-Aware Control Barrier Functions for Collision Avoidance via Bernstein Polynomial Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30696)
*Siwon Jo,Yanze Zhang,Yupeng Yang,Wenhao Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于伯恩斯坦多项式符号距离场（BP-SDF）的几何感知控制障碍函数（CBF），统一表示机器人与障碍物形状，实现安全导航与多机器人避碰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CBF方法使用球体或超椭球体等形状代理时，对不规则几何体过于保守，或需要大量局部基元导致约束数量膨胀，影响实时性能。

Method: 利用伯恩斯坦多项式符号距离场（BP-SDF）统一表示障碍物和机器人，构建几何感知的CBF；利用伯恩斯坦多项式的可微性，在闭环中施加控制约束。

Result: 通过单机器人导航和异构多机器人避碰的仿真实验，验证了该方法在保证安全性方面的效率和性能。

Conclusion: BP-SDF作为统一表示工具，可有效降低CBF的保守性，并保持实时计算性能，适用于不规则几何体的安全导航。

Abstract: Safe navigation often relies on well-defined conditions based on the shape of robots and obstacles, and can be challenging when they have irregular geometries. While Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) offer an efficient mechanism to enforce safe set forward invariance, common shape surrogates (e.g., spheres or super-ellipsoids) either are overly conservative in unstructured scenes or require many local primitives, which inflates constraint counts and degrades real-time performance. In this paper, we introduce a novel geometry-aware Control Barrier Function (CBF) based on Bernstein-Polynomial Signed Distance Fields (BP-SDFs). It provides a unified way to represent the obstacles and robots, so as to represent the barrier function with a unified minimum distance. Benefiting from the differentiability of the Bernstein polynomials, one can easily enforce the control constraints in a closed loop. We validate the method's efficiency and performance to guarantee safety in single-robot navigation and heterogeneous multi-robot collision avoidance via simulations under different environments.

</details>


### [139] [GSAM: A Generalizable and Safe Robotic Framework for Articulated Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30740)
*Beichen Shao,Mengying Xie,Heng Su,Wanyi Zhang,Mingyan Li,Yan Ding,Fausto Giunchiglia,Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GSAM是一个面向服务机器人的通用且安全的铰接物体操作框架，通过视觉感知器估计运动学参数、VLM精炼器利用链式思维进行常识推理修正，以及交互约束函数生成器结合LLM进行轨迹规划，在50个铰链任务中比最优基线降低3.1%标准差并提升36.0%操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法忽略了铰接物体的多样性和末端执行器与把手之间交互的复杂性，导致泛化能力有限且产生破坏性碰撞。

Method: 1) 视觉感知器生成运动学参数；2) 微调VLM精炼器通过链式思维常识推理修正原始估计；3) 交互约束函数生成器整合铰接物体、交互姿态和避障知识；4) LLM将约束函数化并应用于轨迹与姿态规划；5) 运动学感知操作规划器验证可达性。

Result: 在5类物体的50个铰链任务和50个随机初始配置实验中，GSAM相比最优基线标准差降低3.1%，操作成功率提升36.0%。

Conclusion: GSAM在物体泛化能力和交互安全性上均显著优于现有方法，适用于实际场景中的铰接物体操作。

Abstract: Articulated object manipulation is a unique challenge for service robots. Existing methods employ end-to-end policy learning, visionmotion planning, and large-language/visual-language model (LLM/VLM), but often overlook the diversity of articulated objects and the complexity of interactions between end-effector and handle, leading to limited generalization and destructive collisions. To address this, we propose GSAM, a generalizable and safe robotic framework for articulated object manipulation. Specifically, a vision-based perceiver generates the kinematic parameters. Considering that pre-trained markers in perceiver yield raw estimations that may deviate from commonsense, we present a f ine-tuned VLM-based refiner, using chain-of-thought (COT) commonsense reasoning to refine perception. To prevent destructive collisions, we design an interaction constraint function generator, integrating articulated object, interaction pose, and obstacle avoidance knowledge into a base. LLM then functionalize these constraints and apply them to trajectory and posture planning. A kinematic-aware manipulation planner verifies reachability for trajectory and posture. Experiments on 50 hinge tasks across 5 object categories and 50 randomly initialized end-effectorhandle configurations show that GSAM reduces standard deviation by 3.1% and improves manipulation success rate by 36.0% compared to the best baseline, respectively demonstrating the superior object generalization and interaction safety of GSAM in practical scenarios.

</details>


### [140] [SSR: Scaling Surefooted and Symmetric Humanoid Traversal to the Open World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30770)
*Ruiqi Yu,Yiwen Wang,Yuan Hao,Jun WU,Qiuguo Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SSR框架，一种基于自我中心视觉的人形机器人遍历框架，通过想象足部引导、等变对称增强和地形特定运动先验，实现了在多样化真实地形上的安全、稳定、高质量遍历，包括楼梯、宽间隙和高平台等极端场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将人形机器人的遍历能力扩展到开放世界对于实际部署至关重要，但现有方法在视觉引导下的安全足部放置、动态运动协调及自然全身行为方面仍面临挑战。

Method: SSR框架包含三个核心组件：1) 想象足部引导，学习预测摆动脚接触并评估支撑性以引导稳定着陆；2) 等变潜空间对称增强，在高维视觉观测下高效诱导双边协调；3) 地形特定多判别器运动先验，鼓励跨场景的类人行为。

Result: 在多样化真实地形上实现了安全、稳定和高质量的 locomotion，包括不同结构的楼梯、宽间隙、高平台等极端挑战，并在开放户外环境中实现可靠的长时程遍历。

Conclusion: SSR通过端到端学习视觉引导的足部放置、双边协调和类人运动先验，有效提升了人形机器人在开放世界中的遍历能力，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Extending humanoid traversal to the open world is key to practical deployment in human environments, but remains challenging. The robot must use vision to ensure safe and reliable foot placement on heterogeneous terrain under highly dynamic motion, while producing coordinated, natural whole-body behaviors. We propose SSR, an efficient end-to-end framework for egocentric vision-based humanoid traversal that jointly learns these capabilities. SSR introduces imagined foothold guidance, which learns to model forthcoming swing-foot contacts and evaluates their support to guide pre-touchdown swings toward stable regions, reducing edge slips. It further employs equivariant latent-space symmetry augmentation to efficiently induce bilateral coordination under high-dimensional visual observations, and uses terrain-specific multi-discriminator motion priors to encourage human-like behavior across scenes. Extensive experiments show that SSR achieves safe, stable, and high-quality locomotion on diverse real-world terrains, including stairs with varied structures and extreme challenges such as wide gaps and high platforms, while enabling reliable long-horizon traversal in open outdoor environments.

</details>


### [141] [Object-Informed Model Predictive Path Integral Control for Non-Prehensile Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30778)
*Nikola Raicevic,Bharath Raam Radhakrishnan,Chenbin Yu,Ki Myung Brian Lee,Nikolay Atanasov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层MPPI控制方法，通过先求解简化的物体级规划问题，再将其作为参考轨迹引导机器人与物体的联合规划，从而实现非抓取式机器人操作中的高效长时域规划。在6自由度xArm6的推物体任务中，该方法相比常规MPPI在仿真中任务成功率提升40%，控制频率加快26%，真实实验中成功率提升20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非抓取式机器人操作（如推物体）由于欠驱动和不连续的交互特性，长时域规划非常困难。现有方法难以在长预测时域内保持高效和准确的规划。

Method: 提出分层模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制框架：先求解一个简化的仅物体规划问题（假设物体可直接驱动），将得到的物体轨迹作为参考，再求解机器人与物体的联合规划问题，实现物体信息引导的机器人规划。

Result: 在6自由度xArm6机械臂的推物体任务中，仿真环境下任务成功率提升40%，控制频率加快26%；真实实验中成功率提升20%，计算开销与常规MPPI相当。

Conclusion: 物体信息引导的分层MPPI方法能够有效提升非抓取式操作任务的长时域规划性能，在保持计算效率的同时显著提高任务成功率，验证了层次化规划策略在复杂机器人操作中的有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon planning for non-prehensile robot manipulation is challenging due to underactuated and discontinuous interactions. We propose a hierarchical formulation of model predictive path integral (MPPI) control that guides robot-level planning with a separately computed object-level plan to achieve efficient long-horizon prediction. We first solve a simplified object-only problem, assuming the object can be actuated directly, and use the planned object trajectory as a reference in solving the joint robot-object planning problem. We evaluate our method in both simulation and hardware using a 6-DoF xArm6 manipulator to perform object pushing tasks in which the target object must reach a goal while avoiding static obstacles, necessitating non-myopic reasoning. Our object-informed MPPI increases task success by 40\% with a 26\% faster control frequency in simulation, and by 20\% in real experiments with similar computation as regular MPPI.

</details>


### [142] [Two Degree-of-Freedom Vibratory Transport in a Grasp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30780)
*C. L. Yako,Shenli Yuan,Kenneth Salisbury*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文利用非对称振动实现了抓取零件的两自由度（平移+旋转）手内操作，通过闭环位置控制生成周期性粘滑波形，并理论分析了波形参数对速度的影响，实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机械手抓取后难以灵活调整零件位姿，现有振动操作多限于单自由度或依赖复杂机构。本文旨在用简单振动波形实现多自由度手内零件操控，提高操作的灵活性和精度。

Method: 1) 通过闭环位置控制的运动表面产生非对称粘滑振动波形；2) 理论分析粘附加速度和滑动加速度两个波形参数对平均速度的影响；3) 构建2自由度振动表面（单向平移+绕法向旋转）；4) 将两个2自由度表面对置为平行爪抓手，进行抓取零件的双向平移和旋转实验。

Result: 1) 理论分析表明粘附加速度和滑动加速度是影响零件平均速度的关键参数；2) 实验验证了理论趋势；3) 成功实现了对多种抓取零件的双向平移和面内旋转操作；4) 平移的波形规律同样适用于旋转运动。

Conclusion: 非对称振动波形可有效实现抓取零件的多自由度手内操控，该方法结构简单、控制灵活，为机器人灵巧操作提供了新的途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we use asymmetric vibrations to demonstrate two degree-of-freedom (DoF) in-hand manipulation of grasped parts. The asymmetric vibrations are achieved through closed-loop position control of a moving surface, which applies a periodic stick-slip waveform to the part to be manipulated. We show analytically how two vibratory waveform parameters, the sticking acceleration and the slipping acceleration, affect average part velocity when moving against gravity. The theoretical trends are then validated using an experimental setup where the squeeze force is controlled and part motion is recorded by a high-resolution encoder. We also develop a 2-DoF vibratory surface capable of translation in one direction and rotation about the surface normal. Using two of these 2-DoF surfaces in a parallel jaw gripper configuration, we bidirectionally translate and rotate a variety of grasped parts, as well as demonstrate that the same waveform trends for translation also persist for in-plane rotation.

</details>


### [143] [Feat2Go: Visual Feature-Grounded Value Estimation for Embodied Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30795)
*Junyang Shu,Zhiwei Lin,Bingqing Wei,Yongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Feat2Go提出了一种细粒度价值估计框架，通过预训练视觉世界模型提取的渐进式进度目标来重塑奖励信号，在不依赖人工奖励工程的情况下显著提升了VLA模型在强化学习中的表现，在ManiSkill3和RoboTwin 2.0上均取得大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在强化学习中面临稀疏监督和长程操作任务中奖励信号设计困难的问题，缺乏细粒度的进度反馈来引导策略优化。

Method: 首先从预训练视觉世界模型中通过patch级相似度度量提取连续进度目标，并用趋势聚类将轨迹划分为语义阶段；然后训练一个具身价值模型从当前观测和任务指令预测结构化的进度值，并用该值重塑终端奖励，兼容PPO和GRPO等现有RL管线。

Result: 在ManiSkill3上，Feat2Go将OpenVLA-OFT的OOD成功率从17.5%提升至82.9%，同时保留96.9%的ID性能；在RoboTwin 2.0上实现88.8%的平均成功率，优于此前强化学习方法。

Conclusion: Feat2Go通过自动生成的细粒度进度信号有效解决了VLA模型在强化学习中的稀疏奖励问题，无需人工奖励工程即可显著提升策略性能，且兼容现有主流RL框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is a promising approach for improving the capabilities of vision-language-action (VLA) models while avoiding the heavy data requirements of imitation learning. However, its effectiveness for VLA models is often constrained by sparse supervision and the difficulty of designing informative reward signals for long-horizon manipulation. In this work, we present Feat2Go, a fine-grained value estimation framework for embodied reinforcement learning. Specifically, Feat2Go first derives a continuous progress target from a pretrained visual world model by measuring patch-level similarity to subgoal states and partitioning episodes into semantic stages with trend-based clustering. We then train an embodied value model to predict this structural progress from the current observation and task instruction, and use the predicted value to reshape terminal rewards during policy optimization. The proposed framework is compatible with existing VLA policy reinforcement learning pipelines, including PPO and GRPO, and does not rely on manual reward engineering. Extensive experiments on ManiSkill3 and RoboTwin 2.0 demonstrate that Feat2Go consistently improves the performance of existing VLA models under both single-arm and bimanual manipulation settings. More specifically, on ManiSkill3, Feat2Go improves OpenVLAOFT from 17.5% to 82.9% average out-of-distribution success while retaining 96.9% in-distribution performance. On RoboTwin 2.0, Feat2Go achieves an average success rate of 88.8% in domain-randomized task settings, outperforming prior reinforcement learning methods.

</details>


### [144] [Hide-and-Seek in Trajectories: Discovering Failure Signals for VLA Runtime Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30834)
*Seongheon Park,Wendi Li,Changdae Oh,Samuel Yeh,Zsolt Kira,Michael Hagenow,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Hide-and-Seek框架，通过对比学习从轨迹级监督中定位VLA模型的失败动作，无需逐帧标注，在多个基准和实物平台上取得最优失败检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在执行任务时容易发生失败，现有检测方法要么代价高（重采样/外部模型），要么粗粒度（将轨迹级标签均匀分配给每一步），无法捕捉局部失败信号。

Method: 将失败检测建模为粗监督学习问题，设计轨迹间和轨迹内两种对比学习目标，从轨迹级标签中自动定位出指示失败的关键动作，诱导出时间结构化的失败信号。

Result: 在LIBERO、VLABench和实物平台三个基准上，基于OpenVLA、π₀和π₀.₅三种策略，均取得SOTA多任务失败检测性能，在准确率-时效性权衡下表现优异，对已知和未知任务均有良好泛化。

Conclusion: Hide-and-Seek有效解决了VLA失败检测中标签粒度不足的问题，只需轨迹级监督即可实现细粒度的失败动作定位，具有实用性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable robots to follow natural language instructions and generalize across diverse tasks, but they remain vulnerable to execution failures that compromise reliability in real-world deployment. Detecting such failures during execution is therefore critical for the robust deployment of embodied systems. Existing failure detection methods either rely on expensive action resampling or external models, while alternatives propagate trajectory-level labels uniformly across every timestep, obscuring localized failure signals. In this paper, we propose \textbf{Hide-and-Seek}, a framework that formulates VLA failure detection as a coarsely supervised learning problem. By combining inter-trajectory and intra-trajectory contrastive objectives, Hide-and-Seek localizes failure-indicative actions and induces temporally structured failure signals from trajectory-level supervision alone, without any step-level annotation. We evaluate Hide-and-Seek on LIBERO, VLABench, and a real-world robotic platform across three representative VLA policies: OpenVLA, $π_0$, and $π_{0.5}$.Our method achieves state-of-the-art multi-task failure detection performance with a practical accuracy--timeliness trade-off under conformal prediction, and generalizes well to both seen and unseen tasks.

</details>


### [145] [High-Load-Density Electro-Permanent Magnetic Foot with Controllable Adhesion for Quadruped Wall-Climbing Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30849)
*An Li,Bo Tao,I-Ming Chen,Han Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高负载密度电永磁吸附足，用于四足机器人在铁磁表面上的可靠攀爬，实现了超200:1的负载自重比和超过1000N的吸附力，并在实际机器人上验证了天花板、垂直壁面及复杂表面的稳定运动能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足机器人在铁磁表面攀爬时，传统电磁或永磁吸附方案存在负载密度低、气隙敏感、接触条件不确定时吸附不可靠等问题，亟需一种高负载密度、可控吸附且鲁棒性强的吸附足设计。

Method: 提出环形Halbach网络电永磁（CHN-EPM）吸附单元，利用三维磁路结构和聚磁效应实现分布式并联磁路，提升磁通利用率并降低气隙敏感性；设计磁化驱动器与两级脉冲电流控制策略，实现精确可控磁化；引入柔性压力传感器进行接触力反馈，确保可靠吸附切换。

Result: 最大吸附力超过1000N，负载自重比超过200:1；集成到Unitree GO2四足机器人上，成功实现在天花板、垂直壁面以及涂漆、穿孔、曲面等复杂铁磁表面的稳定攀爬运动。

Conclusion: 所提CHN-EPM吸附足兼具高负载密度、良好鲁棒性和可控性，为四足机器人铁磁表面攀爬提供了一种有效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: To enable reliable climbing locomotion of quadruped robots on ferromagnetic surfaces, this paper presents a high-load-density electro-permanent magnetic foot with controllable adhesion, featuring force-feedback circular Halbach-net electro-permanent magnet (CHN-EPM) adhesion units and a magnetization control system. Due to its three-dimensional magnetic circuit structure and flux-concentration effect, the CHN-EPM enables a distributed parallel magnetic flux path with enhanced flux utilization, resulting in reduced sensitivity to air-gap variations and allowing effective adhesion to be maintained even under partial contact conditions. The proposed CHN-EPM generates a maximum adhesion force exceeding 1000 N with a load-to-weight ratio over 200:1. A magnetization driver and a two-stage pulse current control strategy are developed to regulate the excitation current amplitude and duration, enabling accurate and reliable magnetization. By incorporating a flexible pressure sensor for contact force feedback, the system can effectively monitor attachment and detachment states, ensuring robust adhesion switching under uncertain contact conditions. The proposed system is integrated into a commercial quadruped robot (Unitree GO2), demonstrating high-load adhesion on ceiling and vertical-wall surfaces and stable locomotion on painted, perforated, and curved ferromagnetic surfaces.

</details>


### [146] [Wall-OSS-0.5 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30877)
*Ryan Yu,Pushi Zhang,Starrick Liu,Brae Liu,Miracle Kang,Shalfun Li,Lights Shi,Ellie Ma,Ping Yang,Chris Pan,Jerry Chen,Dongxiu Liu,Rain Sun,Miles Guo,Byron Zhang,Hugo Zhou,Zach Xu,Vincent Chen,Harrison Huang,James Wang,Dance Kuzi,Andy Zhai,Hang Su,Roy Gan,Lucy Liang,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Wall-OSS-0.5，一个4B参数的开源VLA模型，证明VLA预训练本身就能产生可直接执行的机器人行为，而不仅仅是提供更好的初始化。模型通过梯度桥接联合训练策略，在零样本和微调后均取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA预训练的证据几乎都是在任务特定微调后报告的，这留下了一个根本性问题：VLA预训练本身是否产生可执行的机器人行为，还是仅仅为下游策略学习提供了更好的初始化？

Method: 基于3B VLM骨干网络，增强动作生成组件，跨20+具身形态预训练，每epoch处理超100万条机器人轨迹。采用梯度桥接联合训练策略，包含三个互补目标：离散动作预测（提供强梯度）、多模态预测（保持视觉语言理解）、连续流匹配（部署时动作接口）。

Result: 零样本下在17任务套件上实现非平凡真实机器人行为；微调后在15任务上达60.5%平均任务进度，超越π_0.5达17.5%；多模态评估证实动作训练未侵蚀视觉语言能力。

Conclusion: VLA预训练不应仅被视为初始化策略，而是一种可直接测试且已有用的机器人能力来源。

Abstract: Large-scale Vision-Language-Action (VLA) pretraining is increasingly adopted as the foundation for robot policies, yet the evidence for pretrained VLAs is almost invariably reported after task-specific fine-tuning.This leaves a foundational question unanswered: does VLA pretraining itself yield executable robot behavior, or does it merely furnish a better initialization for downstream policy learning? We present Wall-OSS-0.5, an open-source 4B VLA built upon a 3B VLM backbone augmented with action-generation components, designed so that pretrained robotic capability is directly measurable on physical hardware.The model is pretrained across more than 20 embodiments, processing over one million robot trajectories per epoch alongside a grounded multimodal corpus. We adopt a gradient-bridged co-training recipe in which three objectives play distinct and complementary roles: discrete action prediction routes strong VLM-native gradients into the backbone, multimodal prediction preserves grounded vision-language understanding, and continuous flow matching serves as the deployment-time action interface. Before task-specific fine-tuning, the pretrained checkpoint achieves non-trivial zero-shot real-robot behavior, completing several tasks, including a held-out deformable manipulation task, at high task progress on a 17-task suite. After fine-tuning, the same checkpoint serves as a stronger adaptation prior, reaching 60.5% average task progress on 15 real-robot tasks and outperforming π_0.5 by 17.5%. Multimodal evaluations further confirm that action training does not erode grounded vision-language competence: the model preserves broad vision-language ability while strengthening embodied grounding. Together, these results reposition VLA pretraining from an initialization strategy to a directly testable, already useful source of robot capability.

</details>


### [147] [Trajectory Planning for Non-Communicating Mobile Robots using Inverse Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30906)
*Nina Majer,Yannick Epple,Xin Ye,Stefan Schwab,Sören Hohmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合轨迹规划与预测的算法，通过逆最优控制估计其他机器人的目标状态，并让每个机器人从他人视角进行自我预测，从而提升多机器人避碰场景的效率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非通信移动机器人在避碰场景中缺乏高效交互的问题，现有方法（如基于恒定加速度估计）效率较低且可能无法找到可行解。

Method: 采用逆最优控制从历史轨迹中估计所有机器人的未知目标状态，每个机器人从其他机器人视角进行自预测，结合估计的目标状态求解联合预测问题，并将预测结果用于规划。

Result: 在2-8个机器人的仿真场景中，中位到达时间比基于恒定加速度估计的方法快9.8%，且从未出现规划或预测问题无法求解的情况。

Conclusion: 所提方法在效率（更快到达目标）和鲁棒性（总能找到可行解）两方面均优于基线方法，适用于非通信多机器人避碰场景。

Abstract: To enable an efficient interaction of non-communicating mobile robots in collision avoidance scenarios, we present a novel combined trajectory planning and prediction algorithm. Inverse optimal control is used to estimate unknown goal states of all robots based on observed past trajectories. Each robot also takes the perspective of other robots in considering self-prediction and solves a joint prediction problem using the estimated goal states. The resulting predictions are then considered for planning. Simulation results of scenarios with 2-8 robots show that the median of the durations until all vehicles reach their goals is 9.8 % faster compared to planning with constant acceleration based estimated goal states. Moreover, the proposed approach never leads to the solver being unable to find a solution to the planning or prediction problem.

</details>


### [148] [Enhancing Human-Likeness in Reinforcement Learning Agents via Hierarchical Macro Action Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30928)
*Usman Nizamani,M. Shaheer Luqman,Fawad Javed Fateh,Ali Shah Ali,Murad Popattia,M. Zeeshan Zia,Quoc-Huy Tran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HiMAQ，一种基于分层宏动作量化的人类化强化学习框架，通过两级向量量化编码人类示教动作，在保持高成功率的同时显著提升行为与人类的一致度，在D4RL基准上优于非分层基线MAQ，且可集成多种RL算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RL智能体虽然性能优异，但行为模式与人类差异大，缺乏可解释性与可靠性。研究旨在弥合这一差距，让RL智能体既最大化奖励，又能产生与人类行为高度对齐的动作序列。

Method: 提出分层宏动作量化方法(HiMAQ)，包含两级向量量化：底层将原始动作映射为细粒度子动作簇，高层将子动作簇聚合为宏动作簇，从而编码人类示范中的动作结构，并集成到RL策略中。

Result: 在D4RL基准测试中，HiMAQ在人类相似度评分上显著超越非分层基线MAQ，同时保持与先前RL智能体相当或更优的成功率；该改进可泛化至IQL、SAC、RLPD等多种RL算法。

Conclusion: 分层量化结构能更有效地捕捉人类动作模式中的层次化结构，从而在保持任务性能的同时提升智能体行为的类人程度，为人机交互与可信AI提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Human-like agents are a long-standing goal of artificial intelligence. Despite strong performance, most reinforcement learning (RL) agents remain reward-driven and often exhibit behaviors that differ from humans, limiting interpretability and reliability. In this work, we introduce a novel human-like RL framework that predicts action sequences closely aligned with human behaviors while maximizing rewards. Specifically, we encode human demonstrations into macro actions using a hierarchical macro action quantization approach (termed HiMAQ) consisting of two successive levels of vector quantization. The lower quantization level maps input actions to fine-grained subaction clusters, while the higher quantization level aggregates these subaction clusters into action clusters. Extensive evaluations on the D4RL benchmarks show that our hierarchical approach outperforms the non-hierarchical baseline (MAQ), achieving better human-likeness scores while maintaining comparable or better success rates than previous RL agents. The improvements generalize across integrations with various RL algorithms, namely IQL, SAC, and RLPD.

</details>


### [149] [RDGen: Demonstration Generation for High-Quality Robot Learning via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30957)
*Zijian Zhu,Menglin Zou,Zhuang Li,Yaojie Tu,Xinhai Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RDGen，一种利用强化学习从仿真到真实机器人生成高质量演示数据的框架，用于替代昂贵的人工遥操作，提升VLA模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型受限于高质量机器人轨迹数据的稀缺性，而人工遥操作采集数据成本高、耗时长、难以规模化。

Method: RDGen包含三个组件：VLM任务解析器、Grounding DINO物体定位器和从仿真迁移到真实机器人的RL策略。利用RL策略生成成功的轨迹作为高质量演示数据，供下游VLA模型训练。

Result: 在pick-and-place任务中，迁移的RL策略实现了高任务成功率。相比人工遥操作，RDGen生成的轨迹更平滑，下游VLA性能更优。

Conclusion: RL生成的演示数据可以作为比人工遥操作更可靠、更一致的监督信号，用于机器人策略学习。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for general-purpose robot control. However, their performance remains fundamentally constrained by the availability of high-quality robot trajectory data. In current robot learning practice, such data are primarily collected through human teleoperation, which is labor-intensive, costly, and difficult to scale. In this paper, we propose RDGen, a sim-to-real reinforcement learning framework for generating high-quality robot demonstrations. Rather than employing reinforcement learning solely as the final control policy, RDGen leverages trained RL policies as a structured trajectory generator. The system consists of a VLM-based task parser that identifies task-relevant objects, a Grounding DINO-based object localizer, and an RL policy transferred from simulation to the real robot. Successful rollouts are then harvested as clean, high-quality demonstrations for downstream VLA training, while the simulation stage further provides a scalable source of additional trajectories at little marginal cost. Experiments on a pick-and-place task demonstrate that the transferred RL policy achieves a high task success rate.
